function fmRecalculateBank(){
 const now=new Set(state.squad||[]);
 const preFirstGW=!state.firstGameweekPlayed&&!(state.pointsHistory||[]).length;

function resolveAutosubs(gw,starters,bench,final=false){const effective=[...starters],autosubs=[];const played=id=>playerPlayedGW(id,gw),canReplace=id=>final||playerClubFinishedGW(id,gw);const sgk=effective.find(id=>pby(id)?.pos==='GK'),bgk=bench.find(id=>pby(id)?.pos==='GK');if(sgk&&!played(sgk)&&canReplace(sgk)&&bgk&&played(bgk)){effective[effective.indexOf(sgk)]=bgk;autosubs.push({out:sgk,in:bgk,reason:'GK no appearance'})}for(const sub of bench.filter(id=>pby(id)?.pos!=='GK')){if(!played(sub)||effective.includes(sub))continue;const misses=effective.filter(id=>pby(id)?.pos!=='GK'&&!played(id)&&canReplace(id));let out=null;for(const miss of misses){const trial=[...effective];trial[trial.indexOf(miss)]=sub;if(legalXI(trial)){out=miss;break}}if(out){effective[effective.indexOf(out)]=sub;autosubs.push({out,in:sub,reason:'No appearance'})}}return{effective,autosubs}}

function resolveAutosubs(gw,starters,bench,final=false){const effective=[...starters],autosubs=[];const played=id=>playerPlayedGW(id,gw),canReplace=id=>final||playerClubFinishedGW(id,gw);const sgk=effective.find(id=>pby(id)?.pos==='GK'),bgk=bench.find(id=>pby(id)?.pos==='GK');if(sgk&&!played(sgk)&&canReplace(sgk)&&bgk&&played(bgk)){effective[effective.indexOf(sgk)]=bgk;autosubs.push({out:sgk,in:bgk,reason:'GK no appearance'})}for(const sub of bench.filter(id=>pby(id)?.pos!=='GK')){if(!played(sub)||effective.includes(sub))continue;const misses=effective.filter(id=>pby(id)?.pos!=='GK'&&!played(id)&&canReplace(id));let out=null;for(const miss of misses){const trial=[...effective];trial[trial.indexOf(miss)]=sub;if(legalXI(trial)){out=miss;break}}if(out){effective[effective.indexOf(out)]=sub;autosubs.push({out,in:sub,reason:'No appearance'})}}return{effective,autosubs}}
function calculateManagerGameweek(gw,snap,final=false){if(!snap)return null;const starters=[...(snap.starters||[])],bench=[...(snap.bench||[])],chip=snap.chip;const {effective,autosubs}=resolveAutosubs(gw,starters,bench,final);let gross=chip==='bench'?[...new Set([...starters,...bench])].reduce((s,id)=>s+latestPoints(pby(id),gw),0):effective.reduce((s,id)=>s+latestPoints(pby(id),gw),0);let captainApplied=null;if(snap.captain&&playerPlayedGW(snap.captain,gw))captainApplied=snap.captain;else if((final||playerClubFinishedGW(snap.captain,gw))&&snap.vice&&playerPlayedGW(snap.vice,gw))captainApplied=snap.vice;const captainMultiplier=chip==='triple'?3:2,captainRaw=captainApplied?latestPoints(pby(captainApplied),gw):0,captainBonus=captainApplied?captainRaw*(captainMultiplier-1):0;gross+=captainBonus;const hit=Number(snap.hit||0),net=gross-hit,contrib={};for(const id of new Set([...starters,...bench]))contrib[id]=0;if(chip==='bench'){for(const id of new Set([...starters,...bench]))contrib[id]=latestPoints(pby(id),gw)}else for(const id of effective)contrib[id]=(contrib[id]||0)+latestPoints(pby(id),gw);if(captainApplied)contrib[captainApplied]=(contrib[captainApplied]||0)+captainBonus;return{gw,gross,hit,net,chip,starters,bench,effectiveStarters:effective,autosubs,captain:snap.captain,vice:snap.vice,captainApplied,captainMultiplier,captainRaw,captainBonus,playerContributions:contrib,provisional:!final}}

state.chips=state.chips||JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT.chips));state.chips.first=Object.assign({},DEFAULT.chips.first,state.chips.first||{});state.chips.second=Object.assign({},DEFAULT.chips.second,state.chips.second||{});state.gameweekLineups=state.gameweekLineups||{};state.lastTransferRollGW=Number(state.lastTransferRollGW||0);
state.completedGameweek=Math.max(META.completed_gameweek,state.completedGameweek||0);state.currentGameweek=Math.max(META.current_gameweek,state.currentGameweek||0);
if(!Number.isFinite(Number(state.bank)))state.bank=100;if(!Number.isFinite(Number(state.lockedBank)))state.lockedBank=Number(state.bank);if(!state.news)state.news=null;state.activeStatuses=state.activeStatuses||{injuries:[],suspensions:[]};
let drawerPlayer=null,drawerContext='transfers',drawerTab='overview',subFrom=null,viewManager=null,lastLeagueIndex=null,teamDisplayCaptain=null,teamDisplayVice=null,starJumpTarget=null,transferSessionSnapshot=null;
let fixtureViewGW=state.currentGameweek||5,starViewGW=META.completed_gameweek||4,teamViewGW=state.currentGameweek||5;
function save(){try{localStorage.setItem('fmfantasy_final_v5',JSON.stringify(state))}catch(e){};if(window.FMCloud)window.FMCloud.queueManagerSave(state)}
function seasonGWMax(){return Math.max(1,Number(META.total_gameweeks||46))}
function seasonHalfCut(){return Math.ceil(seasonGWMax()/2)}
function leagueLabel(){return META.competition||'League'}
function half(gw=state.currentGameweek){return Number(gw)<=seasonHalfCut()?'first':'second'}
function squad(){return state.squad.map(pby).filter(Boolean)}
function countSquad(){const c={GK:0,DEF:0,MID:0,FWD:0},clubs={};let spend=0;squad().forEach(p=>{c[p.pos]++;clubs[p.club]=(clubs[p.club]||0)+1;spend+=Number(p.price||0)});return{c,clubs,spend,bank:Number(state.bank||0),teamValue:spend+Number(state.bank||0)}}
function validateSquad(){const x=countSquad(),e=[];if(state.squad.length!==15)e.push(`Need 15 players (${state.squad.length}/15)`);for(const [p,n] of Object.entries({GK:2,DEF:5,MID:5,FWD:3}))if(x.c[p]!==n)e.push(`Need ${n} ${p}`);if(Number(state.bank)<-0.0001)e.push('Not enough money in bank');Object.entries(x.clubs).forEach(([c,n])=>{if(n>3)e.push(`${c} ${n}/3`)});return{ok:!e.length,e,...x}}
function rulesActive(){return state.teamConfirmed&&(state.firstGameweekPlayed||(state.pointsHistory||[]).length>0)}
function fmMoney(v){return Math.round(Number(v||0)*10)/10}
function fmCurrentPrice(id){return Number(pby(id)?.price||0)}
function fmRecalculateBank(){
 const now=new Set(state.squad||[]);
 const preFirstGW=!state.firstGameweekPlayed&&!(state.pointsHistory||[]).length;
 if(!state.teamConfirmed||preFirstGW){state.bank=fmMoney(100-(state.squad||[]).reduce((s,id)=>s+fmCurrentPrice(id),0));if(preFirstGW)state.lockedBank=state.bank;return state.bank}
 const locked=[...(state.lockedSquad||[])],lockedSet=new Set(locked),base=Number(state.lockedBank??state.bank??0);
 const outs=locked.filter(id=>!now.has(id)).reduce((s,id)=>s+fmCurrentPrice(id),0);
 const ins=(state.squad||[]).filter(id=>!lockedSet.has(id)).reduce((s,id)=>s+fmCurrentPrice(id),0);
 state.bank=fmMoney(base+outs-ins);return state.bank
}

function teamChangesLocked(){const st=gameweekStatus(Number(state.currentGameweek));return state.teamConfirmed&&st.started&&!st.complete}
function startSubstitute(id){if(viewManager)return;if(teamChangesLocked())return alert(`You can't make substitutions mid-Gameweek. Your GW ${state.currentGameweek} lineup is locked until the Gameweek is complete.`);subFrom=id;closeDrawer();renderTeam()}
function completeSubstitute(id){if(teamChangesLocked()){subFrom=null;renderTeam();return alert(`You can't make substitutions mid-Gameweek. Your GW ${state.currentGameweek} lineup is locked until the Gameweek is complete.`)}if(!subFrom||subFrom===id){subFrom=null;renderTeam();return}const a=subFrom,b=id,as=state.starters.includes(a),bs=state.starters.includes(b);if(!as&&!bs){const ai=state.bench.indexOf(a),bi=state.bench.indexOf(b);const ag=pby(a)?.pos==='GK',bg=pby(b)?.pos==='GK';if(ai<0||bi<0){subFrom=null;return renderTeam()}if(ag!==bg){subFrom=null;renderTeam();return alert('The substitute goalkeeper has a fixed goalkeeper bench slot. Reorder the three outfield substitutes instead.')}[state.bench[ai],state.bench[bi]]=[state.bench[bi],state.bench[ai]];subFrom=null;save();renderAll();return}if(as===bs)return alert('Choose a starter and a substitute, or choose two outfield substitutes to reorder the bench.');const next=[...state.starters];if(as)next[next.indexOf(a)]=b;else next[next.indexOf(b)]=a;if(!legalXI(next))return alert('That change creates an illegal formation. Keep 1 GK, 3–5 DEF, 2–5 MID and 1–3 FWD.');state.starters=next;if(as)state.bench[state.bench.indexOf(b)]=a;else state.bench[state.bench.indexOf(a)]=b;if(state.captain&&!state.starters.includes(state.captain))state.captain=null;if(state.vice&&!state.starters.includes(state.vice))state.vice=null;subFrom=null;save();renderAll()}

function validateSquad(){const x=countSquad(),e=[];if(state.squad.length!==15)e.push(`Need 15 players (${state.squad.length}/15)`);for(const [p,n] of Object.entries({GK:2,DEF:5,MID:5,FWD:3}))if(x.c[p]!==n)e.push(`Need ${n} ${p}`);if(Number(state.bank)<-0.0001)e.push('Not enough money in bank');Object.entries(x.clubs).forEach(([c,n])=>{if(n>3)e.push(`${c} ${n}/3`)});return{ok:!e.length,e,...x}}
function rulesActive(){return state.teamConfirmed&&(state.firstGameweekPlayed||(state.pointsHistory||[]).length>0)}
function fmMoney(v){return Math.round(Number(v||0)*10)/10}
function fmCurrentPrice(id){return Number(pby(id)?.price||0)}
function fmRecalculateBank(){
 const now=new Set(state.squad||[]);
 const preFirstGW=!state.firstGameweekPlayed&&!(state.pointsHistory||[]).length;
 if(!state.teamConfirmed||preFirstGW){state.bank=fmMoney(100-(state.squad||[]).reduce((s,id)=>s+fmCurrentPrice(id),0));if(preFirstGW)state.lockedBank=state.bank;return state.bank}
 const locked=[...(state.lockedSquad||[])],lockedSet=new Set(locked),base=Number(state.lockedBank??state.bank??0);
 const outs=locked.filter(id=>!now.has(id)).reduce((s,id)=>s+fmCurrentPrice(id),0);
 const ins=(state.squad||[]).filter(id=>!lockedSet.has(id)).reduce((s,id)=>s+fmCurrentPrice(id),0);
 state.bank=fmMoney(base+outs-ins);return state.bank
}
function transferChanges(){if(!state.teamConfirmed)return{ins:[],outs:[],count:0,cost:0};const old=new Set(state.lockedSquad||[]),now=new Set(state.squad);const ins=state.squad.filter(id=>!old.has(id)),outs=(state.lockedSquad||[]).filter(id=>!now.has(id));const n=Math.max(ins.length,outs.length);const cost=(!rulesActive()||state.activeChip==='wildcard')?0:Math.max(0,n-(state.freeTransfers||0))*4;return{ins,outs,count:n,cost}}
function canAdd(p){if(!p||!p.visible||p.available===false||state.squad.includes(p.id)||state.squad.length>=15)return false;const x=countSquad(),mx={GK:2,DEF:5,MID:5,FWD:3};return x.c[p.pos]<mx[p.pos]&&(x.clubs[p.club]||0)<3&&Number(state.bank||0)+1e-9>=Number(p.price||0)}
function addPlayer(id){fmRecalculateBank();const p=pby(id);if(p&&canAdd(p)){state.squad.push(id);fmRecalculateBank();save();renderAll()}}
function removePlayer(id){const p=pby(id);if(!state.squad.includes(id))return;state.squad=state.squad.filter(x=>x!==id);fmRecalculateBank();state.starters=state.starters.filter(x=>x!==id);state.bench=state.bench.filter(x=>x!==id);if(state.captain===id)state.captain=null;if(state.vice===id)state.vice=null;save();closeDrawer();renderAll()}
function pageInfo(pg){return{transfers:['Transfers',`Build and transfer your 15-player ${leagueLabel()} squad.`],team:['My Team','Manage your XI and browse your own Gameweek history.'],fixtures:['Fixtures & Results','Calendar Gameweeks with doubles, blanks and club filtering.'],news:['News','Transfers, price movements, injuries and suspensions from the latest import.'],star:['Star XI','The highest-scoring XI from each completed Gameweek.'],table:[leagueLabel(),'Authoritative league table from the FM save snapshot.'],leagues:['Mini Leagues','Create leagues and inspect managers’ teams.'],stats:['Stats','Player leaderboards from the FM save.'],rules:['Rules','Scoring, transfers, chips and squad rules.']}[pg]}
function captureTransferSession(){transferSessionSnapshot={squad:[...state.squad],bank:Number(state.bank||0),starters:[...state.starters],bench:[...state.bench],captain:state.captain,vice:state.vice}}
function transferSessionBase(){if(transferSessionSnapshot)return transferSessionSnapshot;const squad=[...(state.lockedSquad||[])];return{squad,bank:Number(state.lockedBank??state.bank??0),starters:(state.starters||[]).filter(id=>squad.includes(id)),bench:(state.bench||[]).filter(id=>squad.includes(id)),captain:squad.includes(state.captain)?state.captain:null,vice:squad.includes(state.vice)?state.vice:null}}
function samePlayerSet(a,b){return a.length===b.length&&a.every(id=>b.includes(id))}
function transferSessionChanged(){const base=transferSessionBase();return!samePlayerSet(state.squad,base.squad)||Math.abs(Number(state.bank||0)-Number(base.bank||0))>.001}
function hasUnreplacedSessionSlots(){const base=transferSessionBase(),quota={GK:2,DEF:5,MID:5,FWD:3},current=squad();return['GK','DEF','MID','FWD'].some(pos=>current.filter(p=>p.pos===pos).length<quota[pos]&&base.squad.some(id=>{const p=pby(id);return p?.pos===pos&&!state.squad.includes(id)}))}
function showPage(pg){const enteringTransfers=pg==='transfers'&&!$('transfersPage').classList.contains('active');if(enteringTransfers)captureTransferSession();document.querySelectorAll('.page').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('active'));$(pg+'Page').classList.add('active');
document.querySelectorAll('.nav').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x.dataset.page===pg));const [t,s]=pageInfo(pg);$('pageTitle').textContent=t;$('pageSub').textContent=s;if(pg!=='team')viewManager=null;renderAll()}
function fmCleanNamePart(x){return String(x||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim()}

 if(!p)return 'Unknown';

 const explicit=fmCleanNamePart(p.canonical_display_name||p.public_nam

 $('bank').textContent=`£${bank.toFixed(1)}m`;$('squadCount').textContent=`${state.squad.length} / 15`;$('freeTransfers').textContent=!state.teamConfirmed||!rulesActive()?'Unlimited':`${state.freeTransfers} / 5`;
 $('transferCost').textContent=!state.teamConfirmed?'Initial squad':!rulesActive()?'Unlimited until first GW played':t.cost?`-${t.cost} pts`:`${t.count} change${t.count===1?'':'s'} · Free`;$('transferCostCard').classList.toggle('warn',!!t.cost);
 const rule=$('transferRule');rule.className='rulebar '+(v.ok?'ok':'bad');rule.innerHTML=v.ok?`Squad valid · current team value £${value.toFixed(1)}m · maximum three from any club.`:esc(v.e.join(' · '));

Changes remain unlimited until that Gameweek starts.`:!rulesActive()?`Your first scoring Gameweek is GW ${state.entryGameweek}. Transfers stay unlimited until its first league fixture is processed.`:state.activeChip==='wildcard'?'Wildcard active — unlimited transfers and saved free transfers are preserved.':`${state.freeTransfers} free transfer${state.freeTransfers===1?'':'s'} available.  $('restoreUnreplaced').disabled=!hasUnreplacedSessionSlots();$('revertTransfers').disabled=!transferSessionChanged();renderChipButton($('wildcard'),'wildcard')

function restoreUnreplacedTransfers(){
 const quota={GK:2,DEF:5,MID:5,FWD:3},original=transferSessionBase().squad,restored=[];
 for(const pos of ['GK','DEF','MID','FWD']){let need=quota[pos]-squad().filter(p=>p.pos===pos).length;if(need<=0)continue;for(const id of original){if(need<=0)break;if(state.squad.includes(id))continue;const p=pby(id);if(!p||p.pos!==pos)continue;if(canAdd(p)){state.squad.push(id);fmRecalculateBank();restored.push(id);need--}}}
 if(!restored.length)return alert('There are no unreplaced original slots to restore.');save();renderAll()
}

 const original=transferSessionBase();state.squad=[...original.squad];fmRecalculateBank();state.starters=[...original.starters];state.bench=[...original.bench];state.captain=original.captain;state.vice=original.vice;save();renderAll()
}

function filteredPlayers(){const q=$('search').value.trim().toLowerCase(),club=$('clubFilter').value,pos=$('posFilter').value,sort=$('sortFilter').value;let arr=PLAYERS.filter(p=>p.visible&&p.available!==false&&(!q||playerName(p).toLowerCase().includes(q)||String(p.club||'').toLowerCase().includes(q))&&(!club||p.club===club)&&(!pos||p.pos===pos));const cmp={price_desc:(a,b)=>b.price-a.price,price_asc:(a,b)=>a.price-b.price,points_desc:(a,b)=>b.fantasy_points-a.fantasy_points,points_asc:(a,b)=>a.fantasy_points-b.fantasy_points,form_desc:(a,b)=>Number(b.form_points||b.form||0)-Number(a.form_points||a.form||0),form_asc:(a,b)=>Number(a.form_points||a.form||0)-Number(b.form_points||b.form||0),rating_desc:(a,b)=>Number(b.avg_rating||0)-Number(a.avg_rating||0),rating_asc:(a,b)=>Number(a.avg_rating||0)-Number(b.avg_rating||0),name_asc:(a,b)=>playerName(a).localeCompare(playerName(b)),name_desc:(a,b)=>playerName(b).localeCompare(playerName(a))};arr.sort(cmp[sort]||cmp.points_desc);return arr}

function playerMinutesGW(id,gw){const p=pby(id);return (p?.history||[]).filter(h=>Number(h.gameweek)===Number(gw)).reduce((s,h)=>s+Number(h.minutes||0),0)}
function playerPlayedGW(id,gw){return playerMinutesGW(id,gw)>0}
function playerClubFinishedGW(id,gw){const p=pby(id);if(!p)return true;const fs=fixturesForClub(p.club,gw);return !!fs.length&&fs.every(f=>f.status==='played')}
function lineupSnapshotForGW(gw,create=false){gw=Number(gw);state.gameweekLineups=state.gameweekLineups||{};const k=String(gw);if(!state.gameweekLineups[k]&&create){state.gameweekLineups[k]={gw,squad:[...state.squad],starters:[...state.starters],bench:[...state.bench],captain:state.captain,vice:state.vice,chip:state.activeChip,hit:Number(state.transferHitThisGW||0)};state.activeChip=null;state.transferHitThisGW=0;state.lockedSquad=[...state.squad];state.lockedBank=Number(state.bank||0);if(Number(state.lastTransferRollGW||0)<gw){if(gw<=Number(state.entryGameweek||gw))state.freeTransfers=Math.max(1,Number(state.freeTransfers||1));else state.freeTransfers=Math.min(5,Number(state.freeTransfers||0)+1);state.lastTransferRollGW=gw}state.firstGameweekPlayed=true;save()}return state.gameweekLineups[k]||null}
function ensureStartedGameweek(){if(!state.teamConfirmed)return null;const gw=Number(state.currentGameweek),st=gameweekStatus(gw);return st.started?lineupSnapshotForGW(gw,true):lineupSnapshotForGW(gw,false)}
function resolveAutosubs(gw,starters,bench,final=false){const effective=[...starters],autosubs=[];const played=id=>playerPlayedGW(id,gw),canReplace=id=>final||playerClubFinishedGW(id,gw);const sgk=effective.find(id=>pby(id)?.pos==='GK'),bgk=bench.find(id=>pby(id)?.pos==='GK');if(sgk&&!played(sgk)&&canReplace(sgk)&&bgk&&played(bgk)){effective[effective.indexOf(sgk)]=bgk;autosubs.push({out:sgk,in:bgk,reason:'GK no appearance'})}for(const sub of bench.filter(id=>pby(id)?.pos!=='GK')){if(!played(sub)||effective.includes(sub))continue;const misses=effective.filter(id=>pby(id)?.pos!=='GK'&&!played(id)&&canReplace(id));let out=null;for(const miss of misses){const trial=[...effective];trial[trial.indexOf(miss)]=sub;if(legalXI(trial)){out=miss;break}}if(out){effective[effective.indexOf(out)]=sub;autosubs.push({out,in:sub,reason:'No appearance'})}}return{effective,autosubs}}

function seasonGWMax(){return Math.max(1,Number(META.total_gameweeks||46))}
function seasonHalfCut(){return Math.ceil(seasonGWMax()/2)}
function leagueLabel(){return META.competition||'League'}
function half(gw=state.currentGameweek){return Number(gw)<=seasonHalfCut()?'first':'second'}
function squad(){return state.squad.map(pby).filter(Boolean)}
function countSquad(){const c={GK:0,DEF:0,MID:0,FWD:0},clubs={};let spend=0;squad().forEach(p=>{c[p.pos]++;clubs[p.club]=(clubs[p.club]||0)+1;spend+=Number(p.price||0)});return{c,clubs,spend,bank:Number(state.bank||0),teamValue:spend+Number(state.bank||0)}}
function validateSquad(){const x=countSquad(),e=[];if(state.squad.length!==15)e.push(`Need 15 players (${state.squad.length}/15)`);for(const [p,n] of Object.entries({GK:2,DEF:5,MID:5,FWD:3}))if(x.c[p]!==n)e.push(`Need ${n} ${p}`);if(Number(state.bank)<-0.0001)e.push('Not enough money in bank');Object.entries(x.clubs).forEach(([c,n])=>{if(n>3)e.push(`${c} ${n}/3`)});return{ok:!e.length,e,...x}}
function rulesActive(){return state.teamConfirmed&&(state.firstGameweekPlayed||(state.pointsHistory||[]).length>0)}

function fmCurrentPrice(id){return Number(pby(id)?.price||0)}
function fmRecalculateBank(){
 const now=new Set(state.squad||[]);
 const preFirstGW=!state.firstGameweekPlayed&&!(state.pointsHistory||[]).length;

function fmCleanNamePart(x){return String(x||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim()}
function fmCanonicalPlayerName(p){
 if(!p)return 'Unknown';
 const preferred=fmCleanNamePart(p.preferred_name||p.known_as||p.common_name||p.nickname);
 const surname=fmCleanNamePart(p.football_surname||p.surname||p.family_name||p.last_name);
 const explicit=fmCleanNamePart(p.canonical_display_name||p.public_name||p.football_display_name);
 if(explicit&&(!preferred||explicit.toLowerCase()!==preferred.toLowerCase()))return explicit;
 if(preferred){
   if(!surname||preferred.toLowerCase()===surname.toLowerCase()||preferred.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).includes(surname.toLowerCase()))return preferred;
   if(preferred.split(/\s+/).length>1)return preferred;
   return `${preferred} ${surname}`;
 }
 const display=fmCleanNamePart(p.display_name);
 if(display)return display;
 const first=fmCleanNamePart(p.first_name||p.forename), normalSurname=surname;
 if(first&&normalSurname)return `${first} ${normalSurname}`;
 return fmCleanNamePart(p.name||p.legal_full_name)||'Unknown';
}

function fmInferActiveSuspensions(payload){const out=[];for(const p of payload?.players||[]){const explicit=Number(p.suspension_remaining??p.suspension_games_remaining??p.ban_games_remaining??0),status=String(p.suspension_status??'').toLowerCase(),until=p.banned_until??p.suspension_until??null;if(explicit>0||/(suspend|ban|unavailable)/.test(status)||until){const bits=['Suspended'];if(explicit>0)bits.push(`${explicit} league match${explicit===1?'':'es'} remaining`);else if(until)bits.push(`until ${fmFmtStatusDate(until)}`);const reason=String(p.suspension_detail??'').trim();if(reason&&!/^active fm suspension$/i.test(reason))bits.push(reason);out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail:bits.join(' · ')});continue}const hist=[...(p.history||[])].filter(h=>h.date).sort((a,b)=>String(a.date).localeCompare(String(b.date)));if(!hist.length)continue;const incidents=[];for(const h of hist){const rc=Number(h.red_cards??h.rc??0),yc=Number(h.yellow_cards??h.yc??0);if(rc>0)incidents.push({date:String(h.date),ban:1,reason:yc>=2?'second-yellow red':'red card'});}let yellows=[];for(const h of hist){const yc=Number(h.yellow_cards??h.yc??0);for(let i=0;i<yc;i++)yellows.push(String(h.date));}for(const [threshold,ban] of [[5,1],[10,2],[15,3]])if(yellows.length>=threshold)incidents.push({date:yellows[threshold-1],ban,reason:`${threshold} yellow cards`});incidents.sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));for(const inc of incidents){const served=fmClubPlayedAfter(payload,p.club,inc.date),remaining=Math.max(0,Number(inc.ban)-served);if(remaining>0){out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail:`Suspended · ${remaining} league match${remaining===1?'':'es'} remaining · ${inc.reason}`});break}}}return out}
function fmInferActiveInjuries(payload){const out=[];for(const p of payload?.players||[]){const raw=p.injury_status??p.injury??p.injury_name??(p.injured?'Injured':null);if(!raw)continue;const text=String(raw);if(/^(fit|none|false|0)$/i.test(text)||/recovered|fully fit|available/i.test(text))continue;const back=p.injury_return_date??p.expected_return_date??p.injury_expected_back??p.injury_end_date??null;const detail=`${/^injur/i.test(text)?text:`Injured · ${text}`}${back?` · expected back ${fmFmtStatusDate(back)}`:''}`;out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail})}return out}

function fmInferActiveInjuries(payload){const out=[];for(const p of payload?.players||[]){const raw=p.injury_status??p.injury??p.injury_name??(p.injured?'Injured':null);if(!raw)continue;const text=String(raw);if(/^(fit|none|false|0)$/i.test(text)||/recovered|fully fit|available/i.test(text))continue;const back=p.injury_return_date??p.expected_return_date??p.injury_expected_back??p.injury_end_date??null;const detail=`${/^injur/i.test(text)?text:`Injured · ${text}`}${back?` · expected back ${fmFmtStatusDate(back)}`:''}`;out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail})}return out}

function fmBuildImportNews(changes,priceMoves,payload,oldP){const oldMap=new Map((oldP?.players||[]).map(p=>[String(p.pid),p])),newMap=new Map((payload.players||[]).map(p=>[String(p.pid),p])),seniorNew=fmFirstTeamNewsIds(payload),seniorOld=fmFirstTeamNewsIds(oldP||{});const transfers=[],seenTransfers=new Set();const seniorEvidence=p=>{if(!p)return false;const ctx=p.price_context||{},played=Number(p.minutes||0)>0||(p.history||[]).some(h=>Number(h.minutes||0)>0),rank=Number(ctx.usage_rank||99),quality=Number(ctx.quality||0),nailed=Number(ctx.nailedness||0);return played||p.first_team===true||/senior|first/i.test(String(p.squad_type||p.squad_role||''))||(rank<=18&&quality>=.48&&nailed>=.35)};const addTransfer=(x,p,from,to,kind='move')=>{if(!p)return;const id=String(p.pid);if(!seniorNew.has(id)&&!seniorOld.has(id))return;if(!seniorEvidence(p))return;if(kind==='added'&&!((p.history||[]).some(h=>Number(h.minutes||0)>0)||Number(p.minutes||0)>0||((p.price_context?.usage_rank||99)<=16&&Number(p.price_context?.quality||0)>=.62&&Number(p.price_context?.nailedness||0)>=.48)))return;const k=`${id}|${from}|${to}`;if(seenTransfers.has(k))return;seenTransfers.add(k);transfers.push({name:x?.name||playerName(p),old_club:from||'Outside league',new_club:to||p.club||'',club:to||p.club||'',pos:p.pos||'',value:fmTransferValue(p),price:Number(p.price||0)})};for(const x of changes?.players_moved||[]){const p=newMap.get(String(x.pid));addTransfer(x,p,x.from,x.to||p?.club,'move')}for(const x of changes?.players_added||[]){const p=newMap.get(String(x.pid));addTransfer(x,p,'Outside league',p?.club,'added')}const active={injuries:fmInferActiveInjuries(payload),suspensions:fmInferActiveSuspensions(payload)};state.activeStatuses=active;return{generatedAt:new Date().toISOString(),gameweek:Number(payload.meta?.latest_gameweek_with_result||0),transfers,priceIncreases:priceMoves?.increases||[],priceDecreases:priceMoves?.decreases||[],injuries:active.injuries,suspensions:active.suspensions}}

function renderNewsCard(id,title,items,type){const el=$(id);if(!el)return;items=items||[];const icons={newsTransfers:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M5 8h13m0 0-3-3m3 3-3 3M19 16H6m0 0 3 3m-3-3 3-3"/></svg>',newsPriceUp:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m4 17 6-6 4 4 6-7m-5 0h5v5"/></svg>',newsPriceDown:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m4 7 6 6 4-4 6 7m-5 0h5v-5"/></svg>',newsInjuries:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect x="5" y="6" width="14" height="14" rx="2"/><path d="M9 6V4h6v2m-3 4v6m-3-3h6"/></svg>',newsSuspensions:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="m6 6 12 12"/></svg>'},preview=items.slice(0,5),rest=items.slice(5),key=id+'Full',head=type==='transfer'&&items.length?'<div class="newsTransferHead"><span>Player</span><span>Old club</span><span>New club</span><span>FM value</span><span>Pos</span><span>FPL price</span></div>':'',activeLabel=type==='status'?'Active now':'Latest import',emptyText=type==='status'?'No active players.':'No changes this import.',search=items.length?`<input class="ctrl newsSearch" data-news-search="${id}" placeholder="Search player or club…">`:'',rows=preview.length?preview.map(x=>newsRow(x,type)).join(''):`<div class="newsEmpty"><span class="newsEmptyIcon">${type==='status'?'＋':'•'}</span><span>${emptyText}</span></div>`,more=rest.length?`<div id="${key}" class="newsHidden">${rest.map(x=>newsRow(x,type)).join('')}</div><button class="btn dark newsMore" data-news-toggle="${key}" data-news-count="${items.length}">View all ${items.length}</button>`:'';el.innerHTML=`<div class="newsHead"><div class="newsTitleWrap"><span class="newsIcon">${icons[id]||icons.newsTransfers}</span><div><h3 style="margin:0">${esc(title)}</h3><div class="muted">${activeLabel}${type==='transfer'?' · senior/first-team only':''}</div></div></div><div class="newsCount">${items.length}</div></div><div class="newsRows">${search}${head}${rows}${more}</div>`}

function fmFirstTeamNewsIds(payload){const ids=new Set();const byClub=new Map();for(const p of payload?.players||[]){if(p.available===false||p.visible===false)continue;const arr=byClub.get(p.club)||[];arr.push(p);byClub.set(p.club,arr);if((p.history||[]).length||Number(p.minutes||0)>0||p.first_team===true||/senior|first/i.test(String(p.squad_type||p.squad_role||'')))ids.add(String(p.pid))}for(const arr of byClub.values()){arr.sort((a,b)=>Number(b.ca||0)-Number(a.ca||0));for(const p of arr.slice(0,18))ids.add(String(p.pid))}return ids}
function fmTransferValue(p){const v=p?.market_value??p?.transfer_value??p?.fm_value??p?.value;if(v===undefined||v===null||v==='')return '—';if(typeof v==='number'){if(v>=1000000)return `£${(v/1000000).toFixed(v>=10000000?1:2)}m`;if(v>=1000)return `£${Math.round(v/1000)}k`;return `£${v.toLocaleString('en-GB')}`}return String(v)}
function fmFmtStatusDate(v){if(!v)return'';try{const d=new Date(String(v).length<=10?String(v)+'T12:00:00':v);if(Number.isNaN(d.getTime()))return String(v);return d.toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:d.getFullYear()===new Date().getFullYear()?undefined:'numeric'})}catch(_e){return String(v)}}
function fmClubPlayedAfter(payload,club,date){return (payload.fixtures||[]).filter(f=>f.status==='played'&&String(f.date||'')>String(date||'')&&(f.home===club||f.away===club)).length}
function fmInferActiveSuspensions(payload){const out=[];for(const p of payload?.players||[]){const explicit=Number(p.suspension_remaining??p.suspension_games_remaining??p.ban_games_remaining??0),status=String(p.suspension_status??'').toLowerCase(),until=p.banned_until??p.suspension_until??null;if(explicit>0||/(suspend|ban|unavailable)/.test(status)||until){const bits=['Suspended'];if(explicit>0)bits.push(`${explicit} league match${explicit===1?'':'es'} remaining`);else if(until)bits.push(`until ${fmFmtStatusDate(until)}`);const reason=String(p.suspension_detail??'').trim();if(reason&&!/^active fm suspension$/i.test(reason))bits.push(reason);out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail:bits.join(' · ')});continue}const hist=[...(p.history||[])].filter(h=>h.date).sort((a,b)=>String(a.date).localeCompare(String(b.date)));if(!hist.length)continue;const incidents=[];for(const h of hist){const rc=Number(h.red_cards??h.rc??0),yc=Number(h.yellow_cards??h.yc??0);if(rc>0)incidents.push({date:String(h.date),ban:1,reason:yc>=2?'second-yellow red':'red card'});}let yellows=[];for(const h of hist){const yc=Number(h.yellow_cards??h.yc??0);for(let i=0;i<yc;i++)yellows.push(String(h.date));}for(const [threshold,ban] of [[5,1],[10,2],[15,3]])if(yellows.length>=threshold)incidents.push({date:yellows[threshold-1],ban,reason:`${threshold} yellow cards`});incidents.sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));for(const inc of incidents){const served=fmClubPlayedAfter(payload,p.club,inc.date),remaining=Math.max(0,Number(inc.ban)-served);if(remaining>0){out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail:`Suspended · ${remaining} league match${remaining===1?'':'es'} remaining · ${inc.reason}`});break}}}return out}
function fmInferActiveInjuries(payload){const out=[];for(const p of payload?.players||[]){const raw=p.injury_status??p.injury??p.injury_name??(p.injured?'Injured':null);if(!raw)continue;const text=String(raw);if(/^(fit|none|false|0)$/i.test(text)||/recovered|fully fit|available/i.test(text))continue;const back=p.injury_return_date??p.expected_return_date??p.injury_expected_back??p.injury_end_date??null;const detail=`${/^injur/i.test(text)?text:`Injured · ${text}`}${back?` · expected back ${fmFmtStatusDate(back)}`:''}`;out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail})}return out}

function fmBuildImportNews(changes,priceMoves,payload,oldP){const oldMap=new Map((oldP?.players||[]).map(p=>[String(p.pid),p])),newMap=new Map((payload.players||[]).map(p=>[String(p.pid),p])),seniorNew=fmFirstTeamNewsIds(payload),seniorOld=fmFirstTeamNewsIds(oldP||{});const transfers=[],seenTransfers=new Set();const seniorEvidence=p=>{if(!p)return false;const ctx=p.price_context||{},played=Number(p.minutes||0)>0||(p.history||[]).some(h=>Number(h.minutes||0)>0),rank=Number(ctx.usage_rank||99),quality=Number(ctx.quality||0),nailed=Number(ctx.nailedness||0);return played||p.first_team===true||/senior|first/i.test(String(p.squad_type||p.squad_role||''))||(rank<=18&&quality>=.48&&nailed>=.35)};const addTransfer=(x,p,from,to,kind='move')=>{if(!p)return;const id=String(p.pid);if(!seniorNew.has(id)&&!seniorOld.has(id))return;if(!seniorEvidence(p))return;if(kind==='added'&&!((p.history||[]).some(h=>Number(h.minutes||0)>0)||Number(p.minutes||0)>0||((p.price_context?.usage_rank||99)<=16&&Number(p.price_context?.quality||0)>=.62&&Number(p.price_context?.nailedness||0)>=.48)))return;const k=`${id}|${from}|${to}`;if(seenTransfers.has(k))return;seenTransfers.add(k);transfers.push({name:x?.name||playerName(p),old_club:from||'Outside league',new_club:to||p.club||'',club:to||p.club||'',pos:p.pos||'',value:fmTransferValue(p),price:Number(p.price||0)})};for(const x of changes?.players_moved||[]){const p=newMap.get(String(x.pid));addTransfer(x,p,x.from,x.to||p?.club,'move')}for(const x of changes?.players_added||[]){const p=newMap.get(String(x.pid));addTransfer(x,p,'Outside league',p?.club,'added')}const active={injuries:fmInferActiveInjuries(payload),suspensions:fmInferActiveSuspensions(payload)};state.activeStatuses=active;return{generatedAt:new Date().toISOString(),gameweek:Number(payload.meta?.latest_gameweek_with_result||0),transfers,priceIncreases:priceMoves?.increases||[],priceDecreases:priceMoves?.decreases||[],injuries:active.injuries,suspensions:active.suspensions}}
function newsRow(item,type){if(type==='price')return `<div class="newsRow" ${item.pid?`data-open-player="${esc(item.pid)}"`:'' } data-news-text="${esc(`${item.name} ${item.club} ${item.pos}`.toLowerCase())}"><b>${esc(item.name)}</b><span>${esc(item.club)}</span><span>${esc(item.pos)}</span><span><b>£${Number(item.old_price).toFixed(1)}</b> → <b class="${item.new_price>item.old_price?'newsPriceUp':'newsPriceDown'}">£${Number(item.new_price).toFixed(1)}</b></span></div>`;if(type==='transfer')return `<div class="newsRow transfer" data-news-text="${esc(`${item.name} ${item.old_club||''} ${item.new_club||item.club||''} ${item.pos||''}`.toLowerCase())}"><b>${esc(item.name)}</b><span>${esc(item.old_club||item.from||'Outside league')}</span><span>${esc(item.new_club||item.to||item.club||'')}</span><span>${esc(item.value||'—')}</span><span>${esc(item.pos||'')}</span><span>£${Number(item.price||0).toFixed(1)}m</span></div>`;return `<div class="newsRow status" ${item.pid?`data-open-player="${esc(item.pid)}"`:'' } data-news-text="${esc(`${item.name} ${item.club} ${item.pos} ${item.detail||''}`.toLowerCase())}"><b>${esc(item.name)}</b><span>${esc(item.club)}</span><span>${esc(item.pos)}</span><span>${esc(item.detail||'Update')}</span></div>`}
function renderNewsCard(id,title,items,type){const el=$(id);if(!el)return;items=items||[];const icons={newsTransfers:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M5 8h13m0 0-3-3m3 3-3 3M19 16H6m0 0 3 3m-3-3 3-3"/></svg>',newsPriceUp:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m4 17 6-6 4 4 6-7m-5 0h5v5"/></svg>',newsPriceDown:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m4 7 6 6 4-4 6 7m-5 0h5v-5"/></svg>',newsInjuries:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect x="5" y="6" width="14" height="14" rx="2"/><path d="M9 6V4h6v2m-3 4v6m-3-3h6"/></svg>',newsSuspensions:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="m6 6 12 12"/></svg>'},preview=items.slice(0,5),rest=items.slice(5),key=id+'Full',head=type==='transfer'&&items.length?'<div class="newsTransferHead"><span>Player</span><span>Old club</span><span>New club</span><span>FM value</span><span>Pos</span><span>FPL price</span></div>':'',activeLabel=type==='status'?'Active now':'Latest import',emptyText=type==='status'?'No active players.':'No changes this import.',search=items.length?`<input class="ctrl newsSearch" data-news-search="${id}" placeholder="Search player or club…">`:'',rows=preview.length?preview.map(x=>newsRow(x,type)).join(''):`<div class="newsEmpty"><span class="newsEmptyIcon">${type==='status'?'＋':'•'}</span><span>${emptyText}</span></div>`,more=rest.length?`<div id="${key}" class="newsHidden">${rest.map(x=>newsRow(x,type)).join('')}</div><button class="btn dark newsMore" data-news-toggle="${key}" data-news-count="${items.length}">View all ${items.length}</button>`:'';el.innerHTML=`<div class="newsHead"><div class="newsTitleWrap"><span class="newsIcon">${icons[id]||icons.newsTransfers}</span><div><h3 style="margin:0">${esc(title)}</h3><div class="muted">${activeLabel}${type==='transfer'?' · senior/first-team only':''}</div></div></div><div class="newsCount">${items.length}</div></div><div class="newsRows">${search}${head}${rows}${more}</div>`}
